vīraśaivānandacandrikā
(vādakāṇḍaḥ)

śrījagadguru
mūrusāviramaṭhagranthamālāyāḥ
aṣṭamaṃ puṣpam
vīraśaivānandacandrikā
śrīmannirañjana maritoṇṭadārya
śivayogiviracitā

belagāṃva śrīcandraśekharamaṭhādhyakṣa �nyāyācārya� �darśanaviśārada� 
śrīguruli"ngadevamahodayaiḥ pariṣkṛtya saṃśodhitā

prathamaṃ saṃskaraṇam

bhrūlatāpuryāṃ 
śrījagadguruga"ngādharasaṃskṛtamahāvidyālayanyāyaśāstrādhyāpakena, 
śrīsomaśekharaśāstriṇā prākāśyaṃ nītā

pṛ0 1) namaḥ śivāya
vīraśaivānandacandrikāyāṃ
vādakāṇḍaḥ

śrīmantaṃ sadguruṃ vande mandā yaddṛṣṭivīkṣaṇaiḥ |
bhajanti sarvavijñatvaṃ sarvavyākhyātṛtāmapi || 1 ||
nānāprameyagahaneṣu mateṣu pāra-
maprāpya saṃśayavaśātparito bhramantīm |
buddhiṃ prasādya varaṣaṭsthalatattvaniṣṭhāṃ
kurvāṇamātmagurumanvahamānato'smi || 2 ||
brahmāhuḥ śā"nkarā yatpaśupatimapare pāñcarātrāśca viṣṇuṃ
karmeḍyaṃ jaiminīyāḥ kaṇabhuganugatāśceśvaraṃ gautamīyāḥ |
avyaktaṃ kāpileyāḥ phaṇipamatavidastvīśvaraṃ tannidānaṃ
svābhinnācintyaśaktisphuraṇasamarasaṃ śrīmahāli"ngamīḍe || 3 ||
svāmin ! vaktravinirgatena bhavato jñeyaṃ samastaṃ kriyā-
kāṇḍenādhigataṃ tataśca hṛdaye harṣaprakarṣo'bhavat |
sotkaṇṭhaṃ mama vīraśaivasumahāsiddhāntavākpaddhatiṃ
nānāvādimatāni tannirasanārītiñca boddhuṃ manaḥ || 4 ||

pramāṇānāṃ jāgarūkatvālli"ngadhāraṇamavaśyaṃ kartavyam ||

tathāhi vātulatantre-

atisthireṇa manasā yāvajjīvaṃ jagadguroḥ |
śivasya li"ngaṃ dhatte yastadetatparamaṃ vratam ||

pārameśvare-

sadā vibhūtisamparkātsadārudrākṣadhāraṇāt |
dhāraṇānmama li"ngasya so'hameva na saṃśayaḥ ||

pṛ0 56) vibhūtiryasya phāle'sti kaṇṭhe li"ngaṃ madātmakam |
rudrākṣadhāraṇaṃ dehe so'haṃ devi na saṃśayaḥ ||
vibhūtirapi rudrākṣaṃ li"ngaṃ yasya trayaṃ tanau |
sa sākṣādrudra īśāni so'hameva na saṃśayaḥ ||

siddhāntaśekhare-

li"ngañca trividhaṃ proktaṃ sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ parātparam |
iṣṭali"ngamidaṃ sthūlaṃ tadbahirdhārayettanau ||
prāṇali"ngantu tatsūkṣmaṃ yadantarbhāvanāmayam ||
parātparantu yatproktaṃ tṛptili"ngaṃ taducyate |
bhāvanātītamavyaktaṃ paraṃ brahma śivābhidham ||
iṣṭali"ngamidaṃ sākṣādaniṣṭaparihārakam |
dhārayedavadhānena śarīre sarvadā budhaḥ ||

vātulatantre-

mūrdhni vā kaṇṭhadeśe vā kakṣe vakṣassthale'pi vā |
kukṣau hastasthale vāpi dhārayelli"ngamaiśvaram ||
dhārayanniyate deśe pavitraṃ li"ngamaiśvaram |
mūtrādyutsarjane yāne bhojane ca sadā śuciḥ ||
gacchaṃstiṣṭhan svapan jāgradunmiṣannimiṣannapi |
śucirvāpyaśucirvāpi li"ngaṃ sarvatra dhārayet ||

ityādyāgamavacanaprāmāṇyānusāreṇa 
mumukṣubhirvīraśaivavrataparāyaṇaiḥ,

tanutrayagatānādimalatrayamasauguruḥ |
dīkṣātrayeṇa nirdahya li"ngatrayamupādiśat ||

pṛ0 57) iti vacanāvagatavedhāmantrakriyārūpabhedabhinnadīkṣātrayeṇa 
sthūlasūkṣmakāraṇaśarīratrayānusyutāṇavamāyeyakārmikarūpamalatrayonmū
lanapūrvakaṃ tanutrayasya tyāgā"ngabhogā"ngayogā"ngatvamāpādya 
gururiṣṭali"ngaprāṇali"ngabhāvali"ngasvarūpamupadiṣṭavāniti 
li"ngadhāraṇasya paramapuruṣārthasādhakatvaṃ prasādhayatsu vacaneṣu 
viśvāsapūrvakaṃ li"ngadhāraṇasyāvaśyakāryatvaṃ, tasya ca 
sārvakālikaśucitvāpādakatvena sadā dhāryatvaṃ, li"ngadhāraṇa 
vratānuṣṭhānaniṣṭhasya sākṣācchivarūpatvaṃ, vratasya ca 
yāvajjīvasambandhitvamityādikaṃ pratīyata ityavaśyaṃ li"ngadhāraṇaṃ 
kartavyam ||
nanvantarli"ngadhāraṇamevābhyarhitam | tathāhi siddhāntasāre-

ārādhayāmi maṇisannibhamātmali"ngaṃ
māyāpurīhṛdayapa"nkajasanniviṣṭam |
śraddhānadīvimalacittajalābhiṣekai-
rnityaṃ samādhikusumairapunarbhavāya ||

siddhāntaśikhāmaṇau-

cidrūpaṃ paramaṃ li"ngaṃ śā"nkaraṃ sarvakāraṇam |
yadasya dhāraṇaṃ citte tadāntaramudāhṛtam ||
cidrūpaṃ hi paraṃ tattvaṃ śivākhyaṃ viśvakāraṇam |
nirastaviśvakāluṣyaṃ niṣkalaṃ nirvikalpakam ||
sattānandaparisphūrtisamullāsakalāmayam |
aprameyamanirdeśyaṃ mumukṣubhirupāsitam ||
paraṃ brahma mahāli"ngaṃ prapañcātītamavyayam |
tadeva sarvabhūtānāmāntarasthānagocaram ||

pṛ0 58) mūlādhāre ca hṛdaye bhrūmadhye sarvadehinām |
jotirli"ngaṃ sadā bhāti tadbrahmetyāhurāgamāḥ ||
dahraṃ vipāpmaṃ paraveśmabhūtaṃ 
yatpuṇḍarīkaṃ puramadhyasaṃstham |
tatrāpi dahraṃ gaganaṃ viśokaṃ 
tasminyadantastadupāsitavyam ||

ityādinā hṛtpuṇḍarīkāntaravartino jotirli"ngasya bhāvanārūpeṇa dhṛtasya 
mānasikairevopacāraiḥ mumukṣubhirdevṛṣibhiryogibhiścopāsyamānatvena 
mokṣapradatvaṃ sarvajanaprasiddhamiti 
taddhāraṇenaiveṣṭasiddherbahirli"ngadhāraṇaṃ na kartavyamiti cenna,

bāhye jāgradavasthāyāmanubhūtārtha eva hi |
antaḥ svapnadaśāyāntu yathaiva paridṛśyate ||
tathāntaryajanaṃ nityaṃ bahiryajanapūrvakam |
bahiryāgavihīnaścedantaryāgo na sidhyati ||
tadbāhyā"nge cāntara"nge paramaprītipūrvakam |
kartavyaṃ sveṣṭali"ngasya pūjanañca manīṣibhiḥ ||
gavāṃ sarpiḥ śarīrasthaṃ na karotyātmapoṣaṇam |
nissṛtaṃ karmaṇā bāhye punastāsāntu bheṣajam ||
evamantaśśarīrasthaḥ parātmā parameśvaraḥ |
vinārcanāṃ bahirdevo hitaṃ na kurute nṛṇām ||

iti vacanādādau saguṇamāśritya paścānnirguṇamāśrayediti 
nyāyeneṣṭali"ngaprāṇali"ngabhāvali"ngānāṃ sakala-sakalaniṣkala-

pṛ0 59) iṣṭali"ngaṃ prāṇali"ngamekīkṛtya yajetsudhīḥ |
maṇimālāṃ mātṛmālāmekīkṛtya japetpunaḥ ||
maṇimāleṣṭali"ngasya mātṛkāprāṇali"ngake |

ityukteḥ prāṇali"ngeṣṭali"ngapūjanaṃ samuccitya kartavyam |

kartavyam ||
nanu li"ngadhāraṇādīnāmetadvacanānurodhena tāntrikairevānuṣṭheyatvena 
śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇeṣvapratipādanena 
cāvaidikatvādvaidikānuṣṭheyatvaṃ nāsti | tathāhi-

vedamārgapracyutānāṃ tantramārgapraveśanam |
strīśūdrabrahmabandhūnāṃ tantramārgādhikāritā ||
śrutipathagalitānāṃ mānavānāṃ hi tantraṃ |
śrutipathaniratānāṃ tatsadā naiva sevyam ||
śrutibhraṣṭaḥ śrutiproktaprāyaścitte bhayaṃ gataḥ |
krameṇa śrutisidhyarthaṃ manuṣyastantramāśrayet ||
tantreṣu dīkṣito martyo vaidikaṃ na spṛśetsadā |
vaidikaścāpi tantreṣu dīkṣitaṃ na spṛśetsadā ||
rājā tu vaidikānsarvāṃstāntrikānakhilānapi |
asaṃkīrṇatayā nityaṃ sthāpayenmatimattamaḥ ||

ityādi smaraṇālli"ngadhāraṇaṃ vaidikairnānuṣṭheyamiti cenna, vaidikānāṃ 
paurāṇikānāñca vacanānāṃ bahūnāṃ jāgarūkatvāt | tathāhi 
śivarahasye-

śivali"nge śarīrasthe śivavattopapadyate |
tacchivānubhavaprepsuśśivali"ngaṃ hi dhārayet ||

pṛ0 60) śā"nkarasaṃhitāyāṃ-

svapnakāle kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ brahmahatyādikaṃ ca yat |
yathā prabodhasamaye tanmithyā bhavati svayam ||
tathā kṛtaṃ mahāpāpaṃ li"ngadhāraṇataḥ purā |
tatsarvaṃ vilayaṃ yāti li"ngadhāraṇataḥ param ||
malli"ngaṃ mastakādya"nge dhṛtvā śakrapurogamāḥ |
devā māṃ li"ngamadhyasthaṃ sampūjya svaṃ padaṃ yayuḥ ||

punastatraiva-

hastasiṃhāsane li"ngamapramādena dhārayet |
pramādātpatite li"nge saha prāṇānparityajet ||

prakaraṇāntare-

kā"nkṣanti sarve pitaraḥ kulīnāḥ 
sambhūya putrāḥ śivabhaktiyuktāḥ |
santo bhaveyuḥ pariśuddhabhāvā
steṣāmathaiko bibhṛyāccali"ngam ||

gāruḍe-

adhamādhamabhāvānāṃ pāpināṃ cābhicāriṇām |
yamalokādbhayaṃ nāsti sadā vai li"ngadhāraṇāt ||

skānde-

ūṃ miti brahmaparamaṃ li"ngākāraṃ maheśvaram |
sarvāvasthāsu sarveṣu kāleṣu nimiṣārdhataḥ ||
aviyogena bhuktiñca muktimicchandvijottamaḥ |
dhārayetpañcasu sthāneṣvasmin saṃlagnamānasaḥ ||

pṛ0 61) ityādi śa"nkarasaṃhitāśivarahasyagāruḍādipurāṇavacanaiḥ 
śrutyarthopabṛṃhaṇarūpairli"ngadhāraṇasya

||

japecca pauruṣaṃ sūktamanyathā rauravaṃ vrajet ||

devīpurāṇe-

li"ngaṃ dvijo mudā dhṛtvā svadehe bhayavarjitaḥ |
sa eva narakasthāyī yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam ||

skānde-

dvijo yadi svadehe tu li"ngaṃ dhṛtvā svakāmataḥ |
sa bhuktvā narakānugrānante māta"ngagatāṃ vrajet ||

śivapurāṇe-

dvijo yaḥ svatanau dhṛtvā li"ngaṃ śudrārpitaṃ mudā |
tasya vai niṣkṛtirnāsti saṃskārairbahubhirnṛpa ||

padmapurāṇe-

śṛṇu rāma mahābāho li"ngaṃcakrādidhāriṇām |

viprasyaitadvigarhyatvātprāyaścittamudīritam |
ādau kṛtvā manaḥkarma ṣaḍabdaṃ kṛchramācaret ||

pṛ0 62) tebhyo gṛhṇan dvijaḥ kāmātsuvarṇaṃ pādameva vā |

pṛ0 64) tathāhi-

pṛ0 65) caraṇaṃ pavitraṃ vitataṃ purāṇam 
yena pūtastarati duṣkṛtāni |
tena pavitreṇa śuddhena pūtā 
atipāpmānamarātiṃ tarema ||

tenā"nkanena pūtāḥ pāpmānamarātiṃ śatruñca atitarema, yena cakrā"nkanena 
pūtāḥ duṣkṛtāni pāpāni taranti taccaraṇaṃ cakraṃ pavitraṃ 
śuddhikāraṇaṃ vitataṃ vistṛtam, purāṇaṃ anādipravāhāgatamiti 
dvitīyamantrārthaḥ ||
mantrāntarañca-

lokasya dvāramarciṣmatpavitraṃ jotiṣmadbhrājamānaṃ mahasvat |
amṛtasya dhārā bahudhā dohamānaṃ caraṇaṃ no loke suhitān dadhātu || iti 
||

asyārthaḥ- lokasya svargalokasya dvāraṃ sādhanaṃ, amṛtasya dhārā bahudhā 
dohamānaṃ nānāprakāramokṣasyāpi sādhanabhūtaṃ, arciṣmat śobhamānaṃ, 
jyotiṣmat jvālākiraṇaprakāśairyuktaṃ, bhrājamānaṃ 
agnitāpanānantaramatyantajvālākalitam, pavitraṃ pariśuddhikāraṇaṃ, 
caraṇaṃ cakraṃ naḥ asmākaṃ loke suhitāndadhātviti ||

pṛ0 67) ādityapurāṇe-

vedeṣu yajñabhāgeṣu yasya nāstyadhikāritā |

nāradīye-

brāhmaṇasya tanurjñeyā sarvadevamayī tataḥ |
sā cetsantāpitā tasya kiṃ vakṣyāmi mahainasaḥ ||

ādityapurāṇe-

brāhmaṇo yadi mohātmā tāpayedvahnimudrayā |

na dāhayed dvijaśreṣṭhaṃ na daheccakrapūrvakaiḥ |
a"nkito yaḥ svadeśāttaṃ rājā śīghraṃ pravāsayet ||
adhyāpane cādhyayane śrautasmārteṣu karmasu |

ātreyadharmaśāstre viṣṇuḥ-

yathā smaśānagaṃ kāṣṭhamanarhaṃ sarvakarmasu |
tathā cakrā"nkito vipraḥ sarvakarmasu garhitaḥ ||
śa"nkhacakraṃ mṛdā yastu kuryāttaptāyasena vā |
sa śūdravadbahiṣkāryaḥ sarvasmād dvijakarmaṇaḥ ||

pṛ0 104) yena svarūpasiddhena sa bhaveddhi nirañjanaḥ |

pādodakaṃ dharedyastu li"ngamūrteḥ śivasya yat |
prakṣālayati tattasya brahmahatyādipātakam ||

brahmāṇḍapurāṇe-

kurukṣetre naimiṣe ca bhūmibhāge ca ye śubhāḥ |
pādatīrthodakasyaite kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
akālamṛtyumathanaṃ sarvavyādhi vināśanam |
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ śambhoḥ pādodakaṃ śubham ||
sarvama"ngalamā"ngalyaṃ sarvapāvanapāvanam |
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ sadyaḥ prāpyate mūrdhni dhāraṇāt ||
pādodakaṃ purabhidaḥ pāśajālaharaṃ param |
pāṇiśuktibhirādāya papuḥ kāmaṃ gaṇeśvarāḥ ||
śambhoḥ pādodakaṃ pītvā paścādaśuciśa"nkayā |
yadyācāmati mohena taṃ vidyādbrahmaghātinam ||

ādityapurāṇe-

pādau prakṣālya devasya karābhyāṃ kamalekṣaṇa |
abhyukṣedātmano mūrdhanyatitheśca gurostathā ||

pṛ0 112) śivarahasye-

matprasādodakaṃ puṇyaṃ sadā dhāryaṃ madāśritaiḥ |
tryakṣā daśabhujā jñeyā mama tulyaparākramāḥ ||

ityādibodhāyanasūtraśivarahasyādityaskāndādipurāṇavacanaiḥ 
śivapādodakasyetikartayatāpūrvakaṃ vidhānāt, 
bhūtalasthitāśeṣatīrthāśrayatvāt, śivasārūpyaprāptisādhanatvāt, 
sakalaroganivartakauṣadharūpatvāt, sarvamahāpātakopapātakanivartakatvāt, 
sakalapramathagaṇasevitatvāt, mumukṣubhiḥ śirasi dhāryatvaṃ peyatvañca 
pratipādyate | tasmādavaśyaṃ śivapādodakaṃ mumukṣubhiḥ sevanīyam ||

nanu-

sālagrāmaśilāvāri pāpahāri viśeṣataḥ |
ājanmakṛtapāpānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate ||

gāruḍe-

pādodakaṃ pibennityaṃ naivedyaṃ bhakṣayeddhareḥ |

agrāhyaṃ śivanirmālyaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam ||
sālagrāmasya saṃsargātsarvaṃ yāti pavitratām |

itivacanaiḥ śivanirmālyasya śivapādodakasya ca sālagrāmanivedanasahitasyaiva

kevalaśivapādodakasvīkāraniṣedhaḥ śrūyate || tathāhi-

sālagrāmādibhiḥ śambhorveṣṭitasya yadarpitam |
tadbhoktavyaṃ dvijairnityaṃ tattoyamapi dhārayet ||

pṛ0 113) śive niveditaṃ bhaktaṃ sālagrāmādyaveṣṭite |

anyathā māṃsatulyaṃ syāttattoyamasṛjā samam |

ityādivacanaiḥ kevalaśivapādodakasya

śivapādodakaṃ na grāhyamiti cenna, kevalaśivapādodakasya bodhāyanena 
grāhyatvapratipādanāt | tathāhi-

athāto mahādevasya pādodakavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |

etadupabṛṃhaṇe purāṇāgamavacanāni, ādityapurāṇe-

pṛ0 114) śivarahasye-

pṛ0 115) iṣṭali"ngapratiṣṭhāyāṃ śrīrudrācchatarudriyāt |

pṛ0 135) bhavatyālalāṭādācakṣuṣorāmūrdhnorābhruvormadhyataśca prathamā

pṛ0 136) bohvorbāhukṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vakṣasā manasā kṛtam |

bhasmaniṣṭhasya sānnidhyādvidravanti na saṃśayaḥ ||

paramāstrañca śaivānāṃ bhasma tatpārameśvaram |

lai"ngye-

smaraṇādeva rudrasya yathā pāpaṃ vinaśyati |

pṛ0 137) yadyakāryasahasrāṇi kṛtvā yaḥ snāti bhasmanā |
tatsarvaṃ dahate bhasma yathāgnistejasā vanam ||

bhasmasnāyī bhavetso'pi sarvapāpādvimucyate ||
bhasmasnānaviśuddhātmā jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ |
matsamīpaṃ samāgatya na bhūyo vinivartate ||
dhṛtatripuṇḍraḥ pūtātmā mṛtyuṃ jayati mānavaḥ |
tripuṇḍraṃ yadyadhṛtvā tu kuryādyatkarma tadvṛthā ||
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ tiryak tripuṇḍraṃ muktisādhanam |
etadāgneyakaṃ snānamuditaṃ paramarṣibhiḥ ||
sarvakarmasamṛdhyarthaṃ kuryādādāvidaṃ budhaḥ |
anyathā sarvakarmāṇi na phalanti kadācana ||

kātyāyanasmṛtau-

śrāddhe yajñe jape home vaiśvadeve surārcane |

varṇānāmāśramāṇāñca mantrato'mantratopi ca |

pṛ0 138) nanu-

jāhnavītīrasambhūtāṃ mṛdaṃ phāle bibharti yaḥ |
bibharti rūpaṃ so'rkasya tamonāśāya vai smṛtam ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ mṛdā sūtre lalāṭe yasya dṛśyate |
sa caṇḍālo'pi śuddhātmā pūjyate na hi saṃśayaḥ ||
yajño dānaṃ tapo homaḥ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛtarpaṇam |
vṛthā bhavati viprendra ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ vinā kṛtam ||
pūjākāle ca home ca sāyaṃ prātaḥ samāhitaḥ |
nāmānyuccārya vidhivaddhārayedūrdhvapuṇḍrakam ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍradharo martyo vartate yatra kutracit |
taddeśa eva viprendra kāśīkṣetrasamo bhavet ||

mārgaśīrṣamāhātmye-

śṛṇu brahman pravakṣyāmi trividhaṃ nāma dhāraṇam |
tulasīmṛtsnayā sārdhaṃ śrīgopīcandanena ca ||
haricandanataḥ kāryaṃ puṇḍraṃ tatra vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
gopīcandanamāhātmyaṃ nibodha gadato mama ||
śrīkṛṣṇatulasīmūlamṛdamādāya bhaktimān |
dhārayedūrdhvapuṇḍrāṇi haristasmai prasīdati ||
yo mṛttikāṃ dvāravatīsamudbhavāṃ 
kare samādāya lalāṭapaṭṭake |
pumān karotīha sadordhvapuṇḍrakam 
kriyāphalaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ tadā bhavet ||

pṛ0 139) kriyāvihīnaṃ yadi kālavarjitam
kṛtvā lalāṭe yadi gopikāmṛdam |
prāpnoti tatkarmaphalaṃ sadā'vyayam 
gopīpateḥ puṇyatamaḥ prasādataḥ ||
gopīcandanasambhavaṃ suruciraṃ kṛtvā lalāṭe dvijo 
nityaṃ dhārayate yadi pratidinaṃ rātrau sadā sarvathā |
yatpuṇyaṃ kurujā"ngale ravigṛhe māghyaṃ prayāge tathā
tatprāpnoti tato'dhikaṃ mama gṛhe santiṣṭhate devavat ||
yasmin gṛhe tiṣṭhati gopicandanam 
kṛtvā lalāṭe manujo bibharti cet |
tasmingṛhe'haṃ nivasāmi sarvadā
śriyā sameto muditaścaturmukha ||
yo dhārayed dvāravatīsamudbhavam 
puṇyaṃ pavitraṃ kalikalmaṣāpaham |
nityaṃ lalāṭe mama mantrapāvitam 
yamaṃ na paśyedapi pāpasaṃyutaḥ ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍraḥ samaḥ saumyo lalāṭe yasya dṛśyate |
sa caṇḍālo'pi pūtātmā pūjya eva na saṃśayaḥ ||
asnāto yaḥ kriyāṃ kuryādaśuciḥ pāpasaṃyutaḥ |
gopīcandanasamparkātpūjya eva na saṃśayaḥ ||
aśucirvāpyanācāro mahāpāpasamanvitaḥ |
śucireva bhavennityamūrdhvapuṇḍrā"nkito naraḥ ||

pṛ0 140) ūrdhvapuṇḍravihīnasya smaśānasadṛśaṃ mukham |
avalokya mukhaṃ teṣāmādityamavalokayet ||
rudrārcanaṃ tripuṇḍrasya dhāraṇaṃ yatra dṛśyate |
tacchūdrāṇāṃ vidhiḥ prokto na dvijānāṃ kadācana ||
na bhasma dhārayedvipraḥ paramāpadgato'pi vā |

ityādipurāṇavacanairūrdhvapuṇḍrasya setikartavyatākasya 
abhyudayaprāpakatayā sarvapāpanivartakatayā ca bahutaraṃ śrūyamāṇatvāt, 
bhasmadhāraṇasya śudrādikartavyatāpratipādanācca, 
ūrdhvapuṇḍradhāraṇameva kartavyaṃ na bhasmadhāraṇamiti cenna, 
ūrdhvapuṇḍradhāraṇādīnāṃ gautama-dadhīci-durvāso-bhṛgu-
brahmopamanyunandikeśvarapramukhadevamuniśāpadagdhadvijaviṣayatvasya bahuśaḥ 
sūtasaṃhitāyādiśrutismṛtipurāṇeṣu 
śrūyamāṇatvādavaidikatāntrikadvijādhamānāmevordhvapuṇḍradhāraṇaṃ na 
tu vaidikānāmiti siddham | ūrdhvapuṇḍradhāraṇaniṣedhaḥ parāśare-

ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ triśūlañca vartulaṃ caturasrakam |
ardhacandrādikaṃ cihnaṃ vedaniṣṭho na dhārayet ||
janmanā labdhajātistu śrautaṃ panthānamāśritaḥ |
puṇḍrāntaraṃ bhramādvāpi lalāṭe naiva dhārayet ||
khyātilābhādisiddhyarthamapi viṣṇavāgamādiṣu |
sthitaṃ puṇḍrāntaraṃ naiva dhārayedvaidiko janaḥ ||
na dāhayed dvijaśreṣṭhaṃ na daheccakrapūrvakaiḥ |
a"nkito yaḥ svadeśāttaṃ rājā śīghraṃ pravāsayet ||
atīva patitānāṃ hi svapadenā"nkanaṃ nṛṇām |
vidhīyate na śuddhānāmataḥ śuddhaṃ na cā"nkayet ||

pṛ0 141) adhyāpane cādhyayane śrautasmārtādikarmasu |
sambhāṣaṇe ca sambandhenādhikārī hi lāñchitaḥ ||

hīnajāterayaṃ dharmo na jātu syād dvijanmanaḥ ||

skānde muktikhaṇḍe-

pāñcarātre ca kāpāle tathā kālāmukhe'pi ca |
bauddhe'pi cārhate caiva vāme pāśupate'pi ca ||

iti gautamaśāpaḥ | sūtasaṃhitāyāṃ yajñavaibhavakhaṇḍe

ityārabhya,

tadjñātvā tu muniḥ śrīmān gautamo munisattamaḥ |
mahākrodhena saṃyuktaḥ śaśāpa brāhmaṇādhamān ||
mahādevasvarūpe ca mahādevasya vigrahe |
mahādevasya bhaktau ca mahādevasya nāmni ca ||
mahādevasya vijñāne mahādevaparigrahe |
mahādevasya ca sthāne mahādevasya kīrtiṣu ||
mahādevapradhāne ca mahādevāśrameṣu ca |
mahādevasya cotkarṣe tripuṇḍroddhūlanādiṣu ||
rudrākṣadhāraṇe rudrali"ngasyaiva tu pūjane |
rudrālayadidṛkṣāyāṃ rudrayātrādikarmasu ||

pṛ0 142) yadyattu rudrasambandhi tatra tatrāpi durjanāḥ |
bhaveta vimukhā yūyaṃ sarvadā brāhmaṇādhamāḥ ||
śrutismṛtipurāṇokteṣvartheṣu nikhileṣvapi |
bhaveta vimukhā yūyaṃ sarvathā brāhmaṇādhamāḥ ||
viṣṇavādidevatāḥ sarvā viśiṣṭāḥ śa"nkarāditi |

bhaveta śraddhayā sārdhaṃ satataṃ brāhmaṇādhamāḥ ||
pāñcarātre ca kāpāle tathā kālāmukhe'pi ca |
śākte ca dīkṣitā yūyaṃ bhavata brāhmaṇādhamā ||
pākhaṇḍeṣu tathānyeṣu mārgeṣvaśrautakeṣu ca |
śraddhayā dīkṣitā yūyaṃ bhavata brāhmaṇādhamāḥ ||
bahunoktena kiṃ sākṣācchive saṃsāramocake |
tatsambandhiṣu sarveṣu śrutismṛtyuditeṣu ca ||
yuṣmākaṃ vaṃśajātānāṃ yuṣmākañca tathaiva ca |
śraddhābhāvaḥ sadaivāstu śāpastīvraḥ kṛto mayā ||

ādityapurāṇe dakṣayajñe brāhmaṇānprati dadhīciśāpaḥ-

eteṣu brāhmaṇāḥ sarve yaddviṣanto maheśvaram |
bhavantu vedabāhyāste pāpopahatacetasaḥ ||
pākhaṇḍācāraniratāḥ sarve nirayagāminaḥ |

sāmbapurāṇe dakṣayajñe dadhīciśāpaḥ-

tripuṇḍradhāraṇe bhasmaguṇṭhane vedamantrite |
bhaveta vimukhā yūya yuṣmākaṃ vaṃśajāstathā ||

pṛ0 143) vedabāhyeṣu tantreṣu pāñcarātrādiṣu dvijāḥ |
bhaveta dīkṣitā yūyaṃ sarvadā brāhmaṇādhamāḥ ||
a"ngeṣu cā"nkitā yūyaṃ śa"nkhacakragadādibhiḥ |
bhavata śraddhayā sārdhaṃ vedabāhyā dvijādhamāḥ ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍre tathā śūle vartule caturasrake |
cihne vedaviruddhe tu bhaveta brāhmaṇādhamāḥ ||

parāśaropapurāṇe dvitīyādhyāye brahmaviṣṇvoḥ parasparaśāpaprakaraṇe 
viṣṇuṃ prati brahmavacanam-

krodhena mahatāviṣṭo brahmā sarvajagatpatiḥ |
śaśāpa viṣṇuṃ garbhastho bhava tvamatimohitaḥ ||
tvadbhaktā brāhmaṇāḥ sākṣāttripuṇḍre bhasmaguṇṭhane |
bhaveyurvimukhā nityaṃ vedasiddhe vimuktide ||
tvadīye pāñcarātre ca tantre bhāgavate tathā |
dīkṣitāśca hare nityaṃ bhaveyurnirbhayāḥ punaḥ ||

skānde kālikākhaṇḍe bhṛgoḥ svapatnīghnaṃ viṣṇuṃ tadbhaktānprati ca 
śāpaḥ-

ekadaṇḍāstridaṇḍāśca ye ca bhaktā hare tvayi |
te'dya pākhaṇḍinaḥ santu draṣṭṛrṇāṃ narakapradāḥ ||
durvāsaso muneḥ śāpātkupitasya mahātmanaḥ |
kaṇvaśāpātbhṛgoḥ śāpādupamanyośca śāpataḥ ||
brahmaśāpāttathā vipra nandikeśvaraśāpataḥ |
dvijānāṃ dākṣiṇātyānāṃ pāpopahatacetasām ||

pṛ0 144) śive bhasmani rudrākṣe kiñcicchraddhā na vidyate |

vaidikācāranirṇaye bhasmamāhātmyaprastāve-

śrutyuktabhasmarudrākṣarudrādhyāyaśivadviṣaḥ |
vedamārgaparibhraṣṭā bhaviṣyanti kalau yuge ||
mahāpātakayuktānāṃ jantūnāṃ pūrvajanmasu |
tripuṇḍroddhūlane dveṣo jāyate sudṛḍhaṃ budhāḥ ||

tatraiva brahmagītāyām-

mahāpāpavatāṃ puṃsāṃ śivajñānasya sādhane |
sarvā"ngoddhūlane tiryak tripuṇḍrasya ca dhāraṇe ||
rudrākṣadhāraṇe rudrali"ngasyaiva tu pūjane |
pradveṣo jāyate nityaṃ śivaśabdajape'pi ca ||

skānde muktikhaṇḍe

yasya syādbhasmani dveṣaḥ tripuṇḍroddhūlane'pi ca |
utpattau tasya sā"nkaryamanumeyaṃ vipaścitaḥ ||
teṣāṃ caṇḍālato janma samyagūhyaṃ vipaścitā |
śrutau caiva mahādeve tripuṇḍre bhasmaguṇṭhane ||
śraddhāṃ na kurute martyaḥ puṇyaleśavivarjitaḥ |
pāpiṣṭhānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ tripuṇḍroddhūlane śrutau ||
mahādeve ca vidveṣaḥ svata eva hi jāyate |
bhasmanā vedasiddhena tripuṇḍrasya ca dhāraṇam ||
vinā vedoditācārāḥ smārtāścānarthakāraṇam |
tripuṇḍradhāraṇaṃ muktvā karmāṇi kurute tu yaḥ ||

pṛ0 145) tenaivācaritaṃ sarvaṃ vyarthañcānarthakāraṇam |
tripuṇḍrahīnaṃ viprasya smaśānasadṛśaṃ mukham ||

cakrā"nkananiṣedhaḥ smṛticandrikāyām-

śrautasmārtaparo vipro nā"nkayeda"ngama"nkanaiḥ |
api viṣṇavādi sambaddhaistaptairvā patito bhavet ||
ajñānādathavā lobhādrāgato vā sudarśanam |
dhatte kulaghnaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā savāsā jalamāviśet ||
cakrā"nkitatanurvāpi rājan li"ngā"nkito'pi vā |
nādhikārī sa vijñeyaḥ śrautasmārteṣu karmasu ||
śa"nkhaṃ cakrañca li"ngañca yo mūḍho dhārayettanau |
pākhaṇḍaḥ sa hi vijñeyaḥ sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ ||
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍrañca manasā'pi na la"nghayet |
śrutyā vidhīyate yasmāttattyāgī patito bhavet ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍrā"nkitāśvatthapatravartulali"ngadhṛt |
tripuṇḍradhāraṇādeva bhasmanā pūyate naraḥ ||
ataḥ kiṃ śa"nkhacakrādyaiḥ śarīre devatāyudhaiḥ |
rudraṃ puruṣasūktañca japettiryak tripuṇḍrakam ||
dhṛtvātha bhasmanā vipro brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati |
svalpaṃ bhasma dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ bahudhā parikīrtitam ||
śrautamekaṃ tathā smārtamaparaṃ paṇḍitottamāḥ |
śrautaṃ bhasma dvijā mukhyaṃ smārtaṃ gauṇaṃ prakīrtitam ||

pṛ0 146) anyaccāpi dvijā bhasma laukikaṃ kevalaṃ param |
śrautaṃ bhasma tathā smārtaṃ dvijānāmeva kīrtitam ||
anyeṣāmeva sarveṣāmaparaṃ bhasma kīrtitam |
sarvā"ngoddhūlanaiścaiva tathā tiryak tripuṇḍrakaiḥ ||
bhasma dhāryamiti proktaṃ munibhiḥ sarvamānavaiḥ |
dhāraṇaṃ mantrataḥ proktaṃ dvijānāṃ munipu"ngavāḥ ||
kevalaṃ dhāraṇaṃ vipra anyeṣāmapi kīrtitam |
agnirityādibhirmantrairjābālopaniṣadgataiḥ ||
saptabhirdhūlanaṃ kāryaṃ bhasmanā sajalena ca |
triyāyuṣeṇa mantreṇa dvijāstiryak tripuṇḍrakam ||
dhāryaṃ proktaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhairdhārmikairvedapāragaiḥ |
medhāvītyādibhirmantrairbrahmacārī dine dine ||
bhasmanā sajalenāpi dhārayecca tripuṇḍrakam |
triyambakena mantreṇa praṇavena śivena ca ||
gṛhasthaśca vanasthaśca dhārayecca tripuṇḍrakam |
ūṃkāreṇa triruktena sahaṃsena tripuṇḍrakam ||
dhārayedbhikṣuko nityamiti jābālikī śrutiḥ |
lalāṭe caiva dordvandve tathaivorasi buddhimān ||
tripuṇḍraṃ dhārayennityaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam |
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍrañca jñānā"ngatvena kevalam ||
āmananti dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ śvetāśvataraśākhinaḥ |
ayamatyāśramo dharmo yaiḥ samācarito mudā ||

pṛ0 147) teṣāmeva śivajñānaṃ saṃsāracchedakāraṇam |
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍrañca māyāpāśanivṛttaye ||
āmananti muniśreṣṭhā atharvaśirasi sthitāḥ |
vrataṃ pāśupataṃ yena samyagācaritaṃ budhāḥ ||
tasya jñānaṃ vijāyeta yena pāpavimocanam |
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍrañca jñānā"ngatvena kevalam ||
āmananti muniśreṣṭhāḥ sākṣātkaivalyaśākhinaḥ |
ato'pi pāśavicchedajñānalābhāya siddhaye ||
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍrañca mumukṣuḥ samyagācaret |
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ caiva tathā tiryak tripuṇḍrakam ||
mahāpātakanāśāya vidhatte vaidikaṃ vacaḥ |
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ caiva tathā tiryak tripuṇḍrakam ||
tapastvenaiva sarveṣāṃ vidhatte vaidikaṃ vacaḥ |
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ caiva tathā tiryak tripuṇḍrakam ||
vratatvenaiva sarveṣā vidhatte vaidikaṃ vacaḥ |
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ caivaṃ tathā tiryak tripuṇḍrakam ||
yajñatvenaiva sarveṣāṃ vidhatte vaidikaṃ vacaḥ |
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ caiva tathā tiryaka tripuṇḍrakam ||
jñānārthenaiva sarveṣāṃ vidhatte vaidikaṃ vacaḥ |
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ caiva tathā tiryak tripuṇḍrakam ||
śivena viṣṇunā caiva vajriṇā brahmaṇā tathā |
devatābhirdhṛtaṃ bhasma tripuṇḍroddhūlanātmanā ||
yakṣarākṣasagandharvasiddhavidyādharādibhiḥ |

pṛ0 148) munibhiśca dhṛtaṃ bhasma tripuṇḍroddhūlanātmanā ||
brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyai rvaiśyaiḥ śūdrairapi ca sa"nkaraiḥ |
apabhraṃśairdhṛtaṃ bhasma tripuṇḍroddhūlanātmanā ||
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ caiva yaiḥ samācaritaṃ mudā |
ta eva śiṣṭā vidvāṃso netare munipu"ngavāḥ ||
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍrañca śraddhayā nācaranti ye | 
teṣāṃ nāsti vinirmokṣaḥ saṃsārājjanmakoṭibhiḥ ||
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍrañca śraddhayā nācaranti ye |
te mahāpātakairyuktā iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ ||
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍrañca śraddhayānācaranti ye |
taiḥ samācaritaṃ sarvaṃ viparītaphalāya hi ||
bhasmadhāraṇamāhātmyaṃ mayā vaktuṃ na śakyate |
guruṇāpi ca he viprāḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi ||
brahmaṇā viṣṇunā caiva rudreṇāpi munīśvarāḥ |
bhasmadhāraṇamāhātmyaṃ na śakyaṃ paribhāṣitum ||
tripuṇḍradhāraṇaṃ muktvā muktimicchati yaḥ pumān |
viṣapānena nityatvaṃ kurute hyātmano hi saḥ ||
tripuṇḍroddhūlane dveṣastadanuṣṭhānavarjanam |
rudrākṣadhāraṇābhāvo jñānānutpattikāraṇam ||

skānde muktikhaṇḍe jñānānutpattikāraṇādhyāye śivavacanam-

vedavedāntavidveṣastadadhyayanavarjanam |
vāmapāśupatādīnāmaśrautānāṃ parigrahaḥ ||

pṛ0 149) pāñcarātrādayaścāpi jñānānutpattikāraṇam |
umādevyā ca lakṣmyā ca vāṇyānyābhirdvijottamāḥ ||
sarvastrībhirdhṛtaṃ bhasma tripuṇḍroddhūlanātmanā |
puṇyaṃ bhasma paraṃ brahma dvayaṃ saṃsāratārakam ||
tayoranyataratsevyaṃ saṃsārabhayabhīrubhiḥ |
tenādhītaṃ śrutaṃ tena tena sarvamanuṣṭhitam ||
yena vipreṇa śirasi dhāryate ca tripuṇḍrakam |

muṇḍakopaniṣadi bhasmadhāraṇavatāmeva brahmavidyādhikāritvamuktam-

teṣāmaivaitāṃ brahmavidyāṃ vadeta śirovrataṃ vidhivadyaistu cīrṇamiti ||

etasyopaniṣadvākyasyopabṛṃhaṇaṃ purāṇe-

śirovratavihīnastu sarvadharmasamanvitaḥ |
api brahmātmavidyāyāṃ nādhikārī na saṃśayaḥ ||

saurasaṃhitāyām-

agnirityādibhirmantraiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ śuddhena bhasmanā |
sarvā"ngoddhūlanaṃ kāryaṃ śirovratasamāhvayam ||
idaṃ śirovrataṃ cīrṇaṃ vidhijñaiḥ śraddhayā punaḥ |
teṣāmeva parā vidyā nānyeṣāmiti hi śrutiḥ ||
etacchirovrataṃ nāma śrutamātharvaṇaśrutau |

skānde muktikhaṇḍe-

etacchirovrataṃ kuryāt sandhyākāleṣu sādaram |
yāvadvidyodayaṃ tāvattasya vidyāḥ khalūttamāḥ ||

pṛ0 150) kaurme-

sraṣṭā sṛṣṭicchalenāha tripuṇḍrasya praśastatām |
sasarja sa lalāṭaṃ hi tirya"nnordhvaṃ na vartulam ||

dharmasārasudhānidhau-

tripuṇḍrakaṃ sadā kuryānmantrapūtena bhasmanā |
vedoktena vidhānena śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt ||

śrutibhāskare-

vanaspatigate some bhasmoddhūlitavigrahaḥ |
taṃ śa"nkaraṃ sakṛdṛṣṭvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||

śrutisārasamucyaye-

aśucirvāpyanācāro manasā pāpamācaran |
śucireva bhavennityaṃ tripuṇḍrasya ca dhāraṇāt ||

mahābhārate-

āyuṣkāmo'thavā rājan bhūtikāmo'thavā naraḥ |
nityaṃ vai dhārayedbhasma mokṣakāmī ca vai dvijaḥ ||

nāradīyasmṛtau-

tripuṇḍraṃ paramaṃ brahma brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakam |
ye ghorā rākṣasāḥ pretā ye cānye kṣudrajantavaḥ ||
tripuṇḍradhāriṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā palāyante na saṃśayaḥ |
tasya vyarthamidaṃ janma yastripuṇḍraṃ na dhārayet ||
bhūtirbhūtikarī puṃsāṃ bhūtiraṃho vināśinī |
vibhūtireṇumātreṇa sarvasnānaphalaṃ bhavet ||
yasya phāle na vā bhūtirnā"nge rudrākṣadhāraṇam |

pṛ0 151) pañcākṣaraṃ na yo jāpī taṃ tyajedantyajaṃ yathā ||

candrikāyāmāhnike-

bhasmasnānaṃ jalasnānādasa"nkhyeyaguṇānvitam |
tasmādvāruṇamutsṛjya snānamāgneyamācaret ||
sarvatīrtheṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvayajñeṣu yatphalam |
tatphalaṃ labhate sarvaṃ bhasmasnānānna saṃśayaḥ ||
nāsti bhasmasamaṃ tejo nāsti bhasmasamaṃ tapaḥ |
ye bhaktāstu mahābhāgā ye dvijāḥ pāpakarmiṇaḥ ||
te'pi bhasmaviśiṣṭā"ngāḥ prayānti paramāṃ gatim |
yaśca phāle vinā bhūtiṃ vinā rudrākṣadhāraṇam ||
na śambhoḥ sadane pūjyaḥ sa vipraḥ śvapacādhamaḥ |
yatīnāṃ jñānadaṃ proktaṃ vanasthānāṃ viraktidam ||
gṛhasthāśramayuktānāṃ dharmavṛddhikaraṃ tathā |
brahmacaryāśramasthānāṃ svādhyāyapradameva ca ||
śūdrāṇāṃ puṇyadaṃ proktamanyeṣāṃ pāpanāśanam |
yasya pa"nktau dvijo bhu"nkte śivabhaktastripuṇḍradhṛt ||
satāṃ pa"nkiṃ punātyekaḥ sakalāṃ caturānanaḥ |
bhasmadhāraṇamāhātmyaṃ sākalyena likhedyadi ||
trilokī tālapatrāṇi nālamāyuḥ śataṃ nṛṇām |

ityādiśrutipurāṇavacanabalādāyurbhūtimuktipradatayā 
āyuṣkāmabhūtikāmamuktikāmadhāryatvasya, bhasmadhāraṇadveṣe

prācīnānekajanmārjitamahāpātakamūlakatvasya, 
bhasmadhāraṇaparityāgapūrvakasakalakarmānuṣṭhānavaiyarthyasya,

pṛ0 152) sambhāvitabrahmahatyādimahāpātakaparihārakaprāyaścittarūpatvena 
sarvavratadānatapoyajñānuṣṭhānarūpatvasya, tripuṇḍradhāraṇānuguṇatayā 
tiryaktvena lalāṭanirmāṇasya sraṣṭustātparyaviṣayatvasya, 
aśucitvāpādakavyāpāradaśāsvapi śucitvaprāpakatvasya, 
bhasmadhāraṇābhāve jñānānutpattikāraṇatvasya, 
rākṣasabhūtapretapiśācādinivartakatvena sakalabhītinivārakatvasya, 
śivaviṣṇubrahmādinikhiladevatādhṛtatvasya, sakalamunijanadhṛtatvasya,

tathā vedetaro mārgo vaidikasya janasya tu ||
tantrāṇi tantraniṣṭhānāṃ rakṣakāṇyapi ca kramāt |

pṛ0 223) nāstītyamalaḥ | nirbījatvānna kiñcidutpādayatītyaprasavadharmā |

sattvodrekātsamādhiprārambhaḥ | ekāgratāniruddhe tu paryantabhūmī | 
pratipariṇāmañca saṃskārāḥ | tatra vyutthānajanitāḥ saṃskārāḥ 
samādhiprārambhajaiḥ saṃskāraiḥ pratyāhanyante | te caikāgratājaiḥ, te ca 
nirodhajanitaiḥ saṃskāraiste ca saṃskārāḥ svarūpañca hanyante | yathā 
suvarṇasaṃvalitaṃ dhyāyamānaṃ sīsakamātmānaṃ suvarṇamalañca nirdahati, 
evamekāgratājanitān saṃskārānnirodhajāḥ svātmānañca nirdahanti | evaṃ 
sa"nkṣepato yogasvarūpaṃ tadupāyāśca veditavyāḥ ||
atha yogābhyāsapradarśanapūrvakā vistareṇopāyāḥ | videhāḥ 
prakṛtilayāśca pūrvamāmnātāsteṣāṃ paratattvādarśanādāvirbhūta eva 
saṃsāre bhavapratyayaḥ samādhiḥ | ataḥ paraṃ tattvajñāne 
tadbhāvanāyāñca muktikāmena yatno vidheya ityetadarthamupadiṣṭam | 
videhaprakṛtilayavyatiriktānāṃ yogināṃ 
śraddhāvīryasmṛtisamādhiprajñāpūrvakaḥ samādhiḥ | tatra yogaviṣaye 
cetasaḥ prasādaḥ śraddhā, vīryamutsāhaḥ, smṛtiranubhūtāsaṃpramoṣaḥ, 
samādhirekāgratā, jñātavyapravivekaḥ prajñā | tatra śraddhayā vīryaṃ 
jāyate, sādhako yogaviṣaya utsāhavānbhavati | sotsāhasya 
pāścātyānubhūtiṣu bhūmiṣu smṛtirupajāyate | tatsmaraṇāccetaḥ

samprajñātasamādherupāyāḥ | tasyābhyāsātparācca

śīghramutpadyate | atha viṣayasukhādikaṃ
